## Maritime Provinces Fishery Regulations {#maritime-provinces-fishery-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/93-55
:::

[FISHERIES ACT](/eng/acts/F-14)

Registration 1993-02-04

Regulations Respecting Fishing in the Provinces of Nova Scotia, New
Brunswick and Prince Edward Island and in Adjacent Tidal Waters

P.C. 1993-188 1993-02-04
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans, pursuant to sections 8,
43[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#footnotestar1_e){#footnotestar1_e-ID0ELBAA} and
46 of the [Fisheries Act](/eng/acts/F-14), is pleased hereby to revoke
the Nova Scotia Fishery Regulations, C.R.C., c. 848, the New Brunswick
Fishery Regulations, C.R.C., c. 844, and the Prince Edward Island
Fishery Regulations, C.R.C., c. 850, and to make the annexed
[Regulations respecting fishing in the provinces of Nova Scotia, New
Brunswick and Prince Edward Island and in adjacent tidal
waters](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-55), in substitution therefor, effective
February 24, 1993.

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#footnotestar1_e-ID0ELBAA){#footnotestar1_e}S.C.
  1991, c. 1, s. 12
  :::
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956710 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations may be
cited as the [Maritime Provinces Fishery
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-55).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956712 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In these
  Regulations,

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Fisheries
      Act](/eng/acts/F-14); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing with a line to which
      one or more hooks are attached and that is held in the hand or
      attached to a rod that is held in the hand or closely
      attended; ([pêche à la ligne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}ou[à la
      ligne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[artificial fly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[artificial fly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a single hook, a
      double hook or two single hooks that are dressed with materials
      likely to attract fish and to which no weight, spinning device or
      natural bait is attached; ([mouche artificielle]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[bag net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bag net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is attached to stakes or buoys,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the bag of which floats with the tide or
        current, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is used to catch fish without enmeshing
        them; ([filet à poche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[brook trout]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brook trout]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any hybrid of brook
      trout, but does not include splake; ([omble de
      fontaine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[clams]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[clams]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes bar clams, bay quahaugs,
      razor clams and soft-shell clams; ([clams]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a calendar day; ([jour ou
      journée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Department of
      Fisheries and Oceans; ([ministère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dip net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dip net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bag-like net that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fixed to a frame to which a handle is attached,
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manipulated by hand to catch fish without
        enmeshing them; ([épuisette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[drag rake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[drag rake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device used in fishing
      for shellfish that is towed along the bed of the water by a
      vessel, but does not include a hydraulic device or a mechanical
      device; ([râteau traînant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[drift net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[drift net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a gill net that drifts in
      the water, whether or not it is attached to a vessel, and includes
      a gill net that is not anchored at both ends; ([filet
      dérivant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 2 of the [Coastal Fisheries Protection
      Act](/eng/acts/C-33); ([bateau de pêche étranger]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net by which fish are
      caught by being enmeshed and that does not enclose an area of
      water; ([filet maillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to angling, means a
      single, double or treble hook, with or without barbs, on one shank
      or shaft; ([hameçon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hydraulic device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hydraulic device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device used in
      fishing for shellfish whereby the shellfish are lifted from the
      bed of the water by the use of water under pressure; ([dispositif
      hydraulique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Indian food fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Indian food fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/93-335, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[inland waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inland waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all waters of a river, stream or brook set out
        in column I of an item of Schedule II that are upstream of the
        boundary or reference point, as the case may be, set out in
        column II of that item, including any waters tributary thereto
        that are upstream of that boundary or reference point, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any case not referred to in paragraph (a),
        all waters that are upstream of the low-water mark at the time
        of low water as set out in the Canadian Tide and Current Tables
        published by the Department; ([eaux
        intérieures]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[jigging]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[jigging]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing by manipulating one
      or more hooks so as to pierce a fish in any part of its body
      instead of luring the fish to take the hook or hooks into its
      mouth; ([pêche à la turlutte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[landlocked salmon]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[landlocked salmon]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means non-anadromous
      salmon; ([ouananiche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[leased oyster area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[leased oyster area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area for which
      a special licence or lease has been granted to a person under
      section 58 or 59 of the Act for the purpose of planting or forming
      oyster beds or for the cultivation and production of
      oysters; ([secteur ostréicole amodié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an eel, the distance measured in
        a straight line from the tip of the nose to the tip of the
        caudal fin,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a salmon or a landlocked salmon,
        the distance measured in a straight line from the tip of the
        nose to the fork of the tail,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a shellfish, the distance
        measured in a straight line through the longest part of the
        shell, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any other fish referred to in
        these Regulations, the distance measured in a straight line from
        the tip of the nose to the tip of the
        tail; ([longueur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[longline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[longline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a line to which two or more
      hooks are attached at intervals and that is anchored to the bed of
      the water; ([palangre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mechanical device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mechanical device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any device or
      equipment used in fishing for shellfish that is not

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} hand-held,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a drag rake, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a hydraulic device; ([dispositif
        mécanique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Atlantic Fishery Regulations,
      1985](/eng/regulations/SOR-86-21); ([maillage]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[open season]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[open season]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a period of time during
      which no close time applies; ([période
      d'ouverture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in the definition
      [[salmon tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, the province of Nova Scotia,
      New Brunswick or Prince Edward
      Island; ([province]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[public oyster-fishing area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[public oyster-fishing area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any area
      that is not a leased oyster area; ([gisement public de pêche des
      huîtres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recreational fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recreational fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing solely
      for pleasure or fishing to catch fish solely for personal
      use; ([pêche récréative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2001-452, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[registration card]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registration card]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fisher's
      registration card or a vessel registration card issued under
      section 5 or under the [Atlantic Fishery Regulations,
      1985](/eng/regulations/SOR-86-21); ([carte
      d'enregistrement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[salmon]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[salmon]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means anadromous
      salmon; ([saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[salmon tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[salmon tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tag that is designed to
      be affixed to a salmon to identify the source and year of capture
      of the salmon and that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is issued with a licence, written permission or
        other document under the authority of which the salmon was
        caught that is issued under

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Act or the regulations made thereunder,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the National Parks Act or the regulations
          made thereunder,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an Act of the legislature of a province of
          Canada or the regulations made thereunder, or

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the laws of any country other than Canada,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is legible, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has not been tampered with; ([étiquette à
        saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[set line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[set line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a line that is not attached
      to a rod or held in the hand and to which only one hook with a
      single point or barb is attached; ([ligne fixe]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[shellfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[shellfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means clams, mussels and
      oysters; ([mollusques et crustacés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[splake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[splake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trout that is a cross
      between a male brook trout and a female lake trout; ([truite
      moulac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sport fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sport fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means smallmouth bass,
      landlocked salmon, salmon and trout; ([poisson de
      sport]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[square net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[square net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net made of wire or
      twine and attached to a pole that is manipulated on a pivot so as
      to lower and raise the net in the water to catch fish without
      enmeshing them; ([carrelet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to a
      county or province, all waters, other than inland waters, that are

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within that county or province, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} adjacent to that county or province and closer
        to that county or province than to any other county or
        province; ([eaux à marée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that is set so as to
      enclose an area of water into which fish are guided through an
      opening or openings by one or more leaders, including what is
      commonly referred to as a box net but not including an eel
      trap; ([trappe en filet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trout]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trout]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Arctic char, brook trout,
      brown trout, lake trout and rainbow
      trout; ([truite]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[weir]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[weir]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      2(1) of the [Atlantic Fishery Regulations,
      1985](/eng/regulations/SOR-86-21); ([bordigue]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[whitefish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[whitefish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means lake whitefish and round
      whitefish. ([corégone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to a species or
  group of species of fish by its common name as set out in column I of
  an item of Schedule I shall be construed as a reference to the species
  or group of species whose scientific name is set out in column II that
  item.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to the weight of
  fish shall be construed as a reference to the weight of the fish in a
  completely unprocessed state.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to a grid reference
  is a grid reference based on the Universal Transverse Mercator Grid
  system expressed in the North American Datum 1927 (NAD 27) used in the
  National Topographic Series Maps, Scale 1:50,000, published by the
  Department of Natural Resources.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise provided or a contrary intention
  appears, a reference in these Regulations to a river, stream or brook
  by its proper name shall be construed to include all waters forming
  part of that river, stream or brook and all waters tributary thereto.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The provisions of these Regulations respecting
  shellfish that apply to specified waters apply also to the foreshore
  of those specified waters.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} In these Regulations, all geographical coordinates
  (latitude and longitude) are expressed in the North American Datum
  1927 (NAD 27) geodetic reference system.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} In these Regulations, the lines connecting points or
  coordinates are rhumb lines, unless stated otherwise.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956789 .Part}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} These
  Regulations apply in Nova Scotia, New Brunswick and Prince Edward
  Island and in Canadian fisheries waters adjacent to those provinces.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/93-335, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fishing for marine mammals;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fishing carried out under the authority of a
    licence issued under the [Atlantic Fishery Regulations,
    1985](/eng/regulations/SOR-86-21);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} cultivated fish found in or taken from

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an aquaculture area or site operated under a
      lease or licence issued by the Minister or under the Fisheries and
      Coastal Resources Act of Nova Scotia or the Aquaculture Act of New
      Brunswick, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a privately owned fish pond that does not
      contain any wild fish and to which wild fish have no access;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} national parks where the National Parks Fishing
    Regulations apply; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a foreign fishing vessel or a person fishing from a
    foreign fishing vessel, except where the vessel or person is engaged
    in recreational fishing.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except for this section and sections 2, 4, 6, 18, 19,
  22, 25 to 29, 34, 35, 39, 56, 58, and 68, these Regulations do not
  apply with respect to fishing and related activities carried out under
  the authority of a licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal
  Fishing Licences Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-335, s. 2
- SOR/97-246, s. 1

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956804 .Part}

### [Licences, Registration Cards and Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956805 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2020-255, s. 10

\]
:::

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsections (2) to (5), no person shall fish for or catch and
  retain any fish unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} authorized to do so under the authority of a
      licence issued under these Regulations, the [Fishery (General)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) or the [Aboriginal
      Communal Fishing Licences
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} accompanying a person referred to in
      subparagraph (i) and is fishing for

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} eels with eel traps,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} gaspereau with gill nets, trap nets, square
        nets or weirs,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} shad with gill nets, square nets or trap nets,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} silversides with trap nets,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} smelt with bag nets, box nets or gill nets,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} sturgeon with gill nets, or

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} tomcod with bag nets, box nets or gill nets;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person holds a fisher's registration card or a
    [[provincial or territorial fisher's
    certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of the
    [Fishery (General) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where a vessel is used in fishing, a vessel
    registration card has been issued in respect of that vessel.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may retain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shad incidentally caught with gaspereau fishing
    gear operated under the authority of a licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/96-125, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} tomcod incidentally caught with smelt fishing gear
    operated under the authority of a licence.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} recreational fishing by angling or with set lines;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} recreational fishing for clams or mussels by hand
    or with hand-held tools;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} recreational fishing for eels, smelt or tomcod with
    spears in tidal waters;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} recreational fishing for smelt with dip nets;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} recreational fishing for gaspereau with dip nets in
    waters other than

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the inland and tidal waters of Prince Edward
      Island, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the inland waters of the Miramichi River and the
      Saint John River;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} fishing for oysters in a leased oyster area; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} fishing for minnows with minnow traps or dip nets.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(b) and (c) do not apply in respect of
  fishing under the authority of a recreational fishing licence or a
  licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(c) does not apply in respect of fishing
  for oysters in a public oyster-fishing area in the inland or tidal
  waters of Prince Edward Island or Nova Scotia.

application for a licence or registration card set out in column I of an
item of the table to this section and payment of the fee set out in
column II of that item, may issue that licence or registration card.

</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">6</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Shellfish replanting
licence</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Free</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">7</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Licence to take oysters
during a close time from a leased oyster area</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Free</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight top">8</td>
<td class="noBorderRight top">Licence to import salmon</td>
<td class="top">Free</td>
</tr>

</table>

</figure>

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-335, s. 4
- SOR/96-3, s. 1
:::

### [Prohibited Species]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956847 .Subheading}

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain or possess Atlantic whitefish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 3
:::

### [Fishing in Inland Waters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956851 .Subheading}

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), no person shall fish in inland waters except by
  angling.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who is
  fishing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for gaspereau, smelt or shellfish;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for minnows with minnow traps or dip nets;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with set lines under Parts XVIII and XIX; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} under the authority of a licence that authorizes
    fishing in inland waters by means other than angling.

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish in
inland waters with the aid of an artificial light or light from a fire.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 4
:::

### [Jigging Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956862 .Subheading}

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish or
assist in fishing by jigging in inland waters or assist in landing fish
that have been caught by jigging in inland waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 4
:::

**[[9.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish by
jigging in the waters set out in column I of the table to this section
during the close time set out in column II.

<figure id="956867">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

<td class="noBorderRight top">Tidal waters of Nova Scotia.</td>
<td class="top">Mar. 1 to Mar. 31</td>

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 4
:::

### [Angling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956875 .Subheading}

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding any open
season set out in these Regulations for angling in any inland waters, no
person shall angle for any species of fish in those inland waters unless
an open season for at least one species of sport fish is in effect in
those waters.

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** No person shall angle in
inland waters

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} with more than one fishing line; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} with a fishing line to which more than three hooks
  are attached.

**[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall angle
in tidal waters for sport fish

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} with more than one fishing line; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} with a fishing line to which more than three hooks
  are attached.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 5
:::

**[[11.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall angle
in tidal waters for any species of fish other than sport fish

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} with more than five fishing lines; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} with a fishing line to which more than six hooks are
  attached.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 5
:::

**[[11.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.3 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, the prohibitions set out in sections 11.1 and 11.2 do not
apply to fishing for commercial purposes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 5
:::

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall angle for sport fish during the period beginning two hours after
  sunset and ending two hours before sunrise.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} angling for smallmouth bass in Micmac Lake or
    Banook Lake in Halifax County, Nova Scotia;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} angling for brown trout in the Cornwallis River,
    Kings County, Nova Scotia or in the Mersey River, Queens County,
    Nova Scotia, downstream from Lake Rossignol to the dam at Cowie
    Falls; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} angling during the period beginning on January 1
    and ending on March 31 in the waters set out in section 116 or
    Schedule IX.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothwithstanding subsection (1), no person shall
  angle in the inland waters of Nova Scotia set out in Schedule IV
  during the period beginning at 22:00 h on any day and ending at
  06:00 h on the following day.

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** Every person who catches
a sport fish by hooking it in a part of its body other than its mouth
shall forthwith return the fish to the water from which it was caught
and, where the fish is alive, release the fish in a manner that causes
the least harm to it.

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** No person shall use a
gaff or spear to land a sport fish that has been caught by angling.

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** No person shall angle or
assist in angling from

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any bridge in Nova Scotia that spans any waters set
  out in column I of Schedule IV; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any bridge in New Brunswick that spans the inland
  waters of or forms the boundary for the inland waters of any river,
  stream or brook set out in column I of Schedule II.

<!-- -->

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall angle for any species of fish in the waters set out in
  column I of Schedule IV by a method set out in column II during the
  close time set out in column III.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The close time established by subsection (1) is
  considered to be fixed separately and independently for each species
  of fish found in those waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 6
:::

### [Possession Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956910 .Subheading}

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess at any time more salmon taken by angling in
  accordance with these Regulations than the applicable yearly fishing
  quota for salmon prescribed by these Regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess at any time more fish of a
  species taken by recreational fishing in accordance with these
  Regulations, except for salmon, than the applicable daily fishing
  quota for that species prescribed by these Regulations.

### [General Bait Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956914 .Subheading}

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** No person shall use as
bait, or possess for use as bait, in a province any

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} live or dead

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} bass, bullhead, sunfish, white perch, yellow perch
    or other spiny fin-rayed fish,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} goldfish or other carp, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} chain pickerel; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} live fish that was not taken in that province.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 7
:::

### [Bait Provisions Specific to New Brunswick]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956923 .Subheading}

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall use as bait or possess for use as bait in the inland waters of
  New Brunswick any live fish.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may use live fish for bait, or possess live
  fish for use as bait, in a body of water set out in section 116 if the
  fish was taken from that body of water and it is not a species of fish
  set out in paragraph 18(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 8
:::

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956929 .Subheading}

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
any species of fish in the waters set out in column I of an item of
Schedule III by a method set out in column II of that item during the
close time set out in column III of that item.

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** Every person who fishes
with a gill net shall set it in a straight line.

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** No person in a province
shall, without lawful excuse, possess any fishing net other than a dip
net.

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess in inland waters or within 15 m of inland waters
  a jigging device or spear.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess in inland waters or within
  15 m of inland waters a snare or bow and arrow unless the person is
  lawfully hunting or trapping under the laws of a province.

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
fishing for gaspereau or shad with a gill net, trap net or weir shall,
during the applicable weekly close time for those fish set out in
Schedules V and VIII, respectively,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} remove the gill net from the water; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} render the trap net or weir incapable of catching
  fish.

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish with
a weir constructed in tidal waters that goes dry at low tide unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the weir contains escape gates for the release of
  fish; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person opens the escape gates before each low
  tide and releases any incidental catch of fish.

### [Spacing of Fishing Gear]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956942 .Subheading}

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided as a condition of a licence, no person shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish for gaspereau in the Gaspereau River, Kings
  County, Nova Scotia, with

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a dip net within 90 m of a square net,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a square net within 60 m of any other square net,
    or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a square net within 60 m of a dam;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fish for smelt or tomcod in waters other than the
  Miramichi River or Miramichi Bay with a bag net, trap net or gill net
  within

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 45 m of any other bag net, trap net or gill net, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 90 m upstream or downstream of any other bag net,
    trap net or gill net;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fish for smelt or tomcod in the Miramichi River or
  Miramichi Bay with a bag net, trap net or gill net within

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 90 m of any other bag net, trap net or gill net, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 180 m upstream or downstream of any other bag net,
    trap net or gill net;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} fish for smelt or tomcod with a bag net, trap net or
  gill net within 90 m of a bridge; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in any case not provided for in paragraphs (a) to
  (d), fish with or set any fishing gear, other than angling gear, a dip
  net, a minnow trap or a spear, within 200 m of any fishing gear
  previously set.

### [Tending of Fishing Gear]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956956 .Subheading}

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** No person shall leave
fishing gear unattended in the water for more than 72 consecutive hours.

### [Temporary Shelters on Ice]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956958 .Subheading}

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, in the provinces of Prince Edward Island and Nova
  Scotia, place a shack or temporary shelter on the ice over any waters,
  unless the name and address of the owner is painted on or otherwise
  affixed to the shack or temporary shelter.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The letters and numerals forming the name and address
  of the owner of a shack or temporary shelter referred to in
  subsection (1) shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} legible;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not less than 50 mm in height; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a colour that contrasts with their background.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall place a shack or temporary shelter on
  the ice before January 1 in any waters set out in section 116 or
  Schedule IX.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every shack or temporary shelter placed on the ice
  shall be removed by the owner by midnight on April 2, in any year, or,
  depending on ice conditions, by the earlier or later date as may be
  specified by a fishery officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 9
:::

### [Taking Shellfish for Replanting]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956969 .Subheading}

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding any close
time or size limit set out in these Regulations, a person may, under the
authority of a shellfish replanting licence, fish for shellfish for the
purpose of relocating them to improve their growth, condition or
accessibility.

## [PART I.1]{.HLabel1}[Inshore and Coastal Licences]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1275331 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1290115 .Subheading}

- **[[29.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this Part.

  [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
      paragraph (a) of the definition [[related
      persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 251(2) of the
      [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([membre de la
      famille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inshore family fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inshore family fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      corporation that operates an inshore fishing enterprise and that
      meets the following conditions:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its voting shares are held by a licence
        holder;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sole director is the licence holder or, if
        the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of which
        belong to one individual, that individual; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its non-voting shares, if any, are held
        by

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a member of the licence holder's family or,
          if the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of
          which belong to one individual, a member of that individual's
          family, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing corporation or an inshore
          fishing family trust. ([société familiale de pêche
          côtière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inshore fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inshore fishing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      corporation that meets the following conditions:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its voting shares are held by a licence
        holder;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sole director is the licence holder or, if
        the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of which
        belong to one individual, that individual; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 100% of its non-voting shares, if any, are held
        by

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a member of the licence holder's family or,
          if the licence holder is a corporation, all of the shares of
          which belong to one individual, a member of that individual's
          family, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing corporation or an inshore
          fishing family trust. ([société de pêche
          côtière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inshore fishing family trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inshore fishing family trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trust

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that has as its sole trustee a licence holder
        or, if the licence holder is a corporation all of the shares of
        which belong to one individual, that individual; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which each beneficiary must be either a
        member of the licence holder's family or, if the licence holder
        is a corporation all of the shares of which belong to one
        individual, a member of that individual's family or an inshore
        fishing company. ([fiducie familiale de pêche
        côtière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definition [[family
  member]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, common-law partnership is to be read
  as a person who is cohabiting with a licence holder in a relationship
  of a conjugal nature and has done so for a period of at least one
  year.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 8](#1275023-1290136){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275023-1290136.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Eligibility Criteria]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1275332 .Subheading}

**[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to
the following licences:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence that is held by a licence
  holder that is identified as an independent core licence holder with
  the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans Canada;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a coastal fishing licence, other than a commercial
  fishing licence to fish for elver;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence with "designated operator
  status" or "[exploitant désigné]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or an
  equivalent expression appearing on it and that is held by a licence
  holder referred to in paragraph (a);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence that is held by a licence
  holder that is identified as a head of a non-core enterprise with the
  Minister of Fisheries and Oceans Canada;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence with "designated operator
  status" or "[exploitant désigné]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or an
  equivalent expression appearing on it and that is held by a licence
  holder referred to in paragraph (d);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence held by an organization
  that has been issued an allocation of fish to catch for the benefit of
  its membership; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} an inshore fishing licence held by a corporation that
  held such a licence before January 19, 1989, unless

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "designated operator status" or "[exploitant
    désigné]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or an equivalent expression appears
    on the licence, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the corporation held an inshore fishing licence
    before January 1, 1979.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 7](#1275021-1275344){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275021-1275344.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[29.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence referred to in paragraphs 29.1(a) to (f) may only be issued to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual or their estate or succession;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a corporation, all of the shares of which belong to
    one individual; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an organization referred to in paragraph 29.1(f).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a licence referred to in paragraph
  29.1(a), (b), (d) or (g), the activities authorized under the licence
  must be carried out personally by the licence holder, the operator
  named in the licence or a person authorized in accordance with
  subsection 23(2) of the [Fishery (General)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A licence referred to in paragraph 29.1(a) or (c)
  shall not be issued if, at the time of application, the applicant has
  transferred the use or control of the rights or privileges conferred
  under the licence for which the applicant is the licence holder or was
  the licence holder in the 12 months prior to the application or
  conferred under a licence that may be issued to the applicant.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A licence holder that has been denied the issuance of
  a licence under subsection (3) or has had a licence cancelled under
  paragraph 9(1)(b) of the Act and did not resolve the matter that gave
  rise to the denial, suspension or cancellation within 12 months of the
  day on which the licence was denied, suspended or cancelled is never
  eligible to hold the same type of licence again.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply in the following
  circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence is offered as security in a financial
    agreement under provincial legislation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph (a), a
    creditor that is a recognized financial institution uses or controls
    the rights or privileges under the licence in order to exercise its
    rights with respect to a security;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph (a), a
    creditor that is not a recognized financial institution, uses the
    privilege under the licence to recommend to the Minister the next
    licence holder or the reallocation of fishing quota, in order to
    exercise its rights with respect to a security;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a trustee or receiver appointed under the
    [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3) uses or controls
    rights or privileges conferred by the licence;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the rights of the proceeds from the
    sale of the catch are transferred to any person on the vessel who is
    participating in making the catch;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an applicant that is a licence
    holder, the rights and privileges under that licence or the rights
    and privileges to be granted if the application is accepted have
    been transferred to an inshore family fishing corporation of which
    the applicant holds 100% of the voting shares;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an applicant that is not a licence
    holder, the rights and privileges that are granted under the licence
    if the application is accepted have been transferred to a
    corporation that would be an inshore family fishing corporation of
    which the applicant holds 100% of the voting shares if the licence
    was issued;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} with the authorization of the Minister, the licence
    holder transfers the rights or privileges under the licence in order
    to give effect to the following arrangements made with other holders
    of the licences referred to in any of paragraphs 29.1(a) to (g):

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an arrangement for the reallocation of a fishing
      quota, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an arrangement for the reallocation of
      authorized fishing gear;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an organization referred to in paragraph 29.1(f)
    has required the licence holder to provide part of the proceeds from
    the sale of the catch back to the organization in exchange for
    additional fish allocation;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a liquidator of the succession, an executor or an
    administrator of the estate appointed upon the death of the licence
    holder uses or controls the rights or privileges under the licence;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a person is authorized to act on behalf of the
    licence holder in the case of their incapacity, including a
    guardian, committee, Public Guardian and Trustee, tutor, curator,
    Public Curator, mandatary under a protection mandate or any other
    person who is appointed to act in a similar capacity; or

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a person has received an authorization in
    accordance with subsection 23(2) of the [Fishery (General)
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) with respect to the
    licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 7](#1275021-1275352){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275021-1275352.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 9](#1275025-1290154){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275025-1290154.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29.2-20201123.html "Link to previous version of section 29.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Requirements for Certain Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1290155 .Subheading}

**[[29.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.3 .sectionLabel}** A licence holder
referred to in paragraph 29.1(a) or (c) shall keep a registry of the
crew on board the vessel on each fishing trip.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 10](#1275027-1290158){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275027-1290158.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[29.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.4 .sectionLabel}** Except in the cases
referred to in subsection 29.2(5), a holder of a licence referred to in
paragraph 29.1(a) or (c) shall not transfer the use or control of the
rights or privileges conferred under that licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 10](#1275027-1290161){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275027-1290161.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[29.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.5 .sectionLabel}** Except in the cases
referred to in subsection 29.2(5), only the holder of a licence referred
to in paragraph 29.1(a) or (c) shall use or control the rights and
privileges conferred under that licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-246, s. 10](#1275027-1290164){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1275027-1290164.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Burbot]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956971 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956972 .Subheading}

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of recreational fishing for burbot

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 10
:::

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956978 .Subheading}

**[[30.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for burbot except by angling.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 10
:::

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956982 .Subheading}

**[[30.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for burbot in the waters set out in column I of the table to this
section during the close time set out in column II.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956992 .Subheading}

**[[30.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.3 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch
and retain in any day from the waters set out in column I of the table
to section 30.2

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} more burbot than the fishing quota set out in column
  III; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any burbot the length of which is less than the
  minimum length set out in column IV, or more than the maximum length
  set out in column V.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 10
:::

## [PART II.1]{.HLabel1}[Chain Pickerel]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956998 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956999 .Subheading}

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of recreational fishing for chain pickerel

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 10
:::

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957005 .Subheading}

**[[31.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for chain pickerel except by angling.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 10
:::

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957009 .Subheading}

**[[31.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for chain pickerel in the waters set out in column I of the table to
this section during the close time set out in column II.

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Clams]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957025 .Part}

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957026 .Subheading}

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall engage in recreational fishing for a species set out in
  column II of the table to this section in the waters set out in column
  I by a method set out in column III during the close time set out in
  column IV.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in fishing for commercial
  purposes for a species set out in column II of the table to this
  section in the waters set out in column I by a method set out in
  column III during the close time set out in column IV.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957038 .Subheading}

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for clams by hand or with hand-held tools shall
catch and retain, in any day, more than 300 clams in the aggregate from
any or all of the following species:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} bar clam;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} bay quahaug;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} razor clam; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} soft-shell clam.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 12
:::

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain from the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to
this section any clam of a species set out in column II of that item the
length of which is less than the minimum length for that species set out
in column III of that item.

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Eels]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957053 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957054 .Subheading}

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[eel pot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eel pot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of fishing gear used to
    catch eels without enmeshing them that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contains bait which attracts eels into it through
      one or more openings, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not have a leader of any kind; ([nasse à
      anguille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eel trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eel trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any fishing gear used to
    catch eels without enmeshing them and includes a fyke net and eel
    weir but does not include a dip net, eel pot, longline, set line,
    spear or angling gear; ([trappe à anguille]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[elver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[elver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an eel that is less than 10 cm
    in length. ([civelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957061 .Subheading}

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
eels except by angling or with an eel pot, eel trap, dip net, longline,
set line or spear.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957063 .Subheading}

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(0.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite any provision in these
  Regulations, no person shall engage in recreational fishing for
  elvers.

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in recreational fishing for
  eels in the waters set out in column I of the table to this subsection
  by a method set out in column II during the close time set out in
  column III.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in fishing for commercial
  purposes for eels in the waters set out in column I of the table to
  subsection (1) by a method set out in column II during the close time
  set out in column III.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1.1) does not apply to fishing for
  commercial purposes for elvers.

- [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in fishing for commercial
  purposes for elvers in the waters set out in column I of the table to
  this subsection by a method set out in column II during the close time
  set out in column III.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who fishes for eels, other than elvers,
  with an eel trap in the inland waters of Nova Scotia shall, during the
  period beginning at sunrise and ending at sunset,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an eel trap other than a fyke net,
    provide an opening at least 90 cm wide in the part of the trap that
    is situated in the deepest water to allow fish to escape; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a fyke net, render the net incapable
    of catching fish.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957078 .Subheading}

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for eels by any method in the waters set out in
column I of the table to this section shall catch and retain, in any
day, more eels than the fishing quota set out in column II.

- **[[38.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall catch and retain any eel from the waters set out in column I of
  the table to subsection 37(1) the length of which is less than the
  minimum length set out in column IV.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who holds
  an eel fishing licence that specifically authorizes the taking of
  elvers.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 14

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Gaspereau]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957093 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957094 .Subheading}

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[dip stand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dip stand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a location specified in a
    commercial fishing licence where the licence holder is permitted to
    take gaspereau with a dip net; ([poste de pêche à
    l'épuisette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[square net fixture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[square net fixture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a structure
    consisting of three walls that is placed in the water and into which
    a square net is lowered and raised to catch
    gaspereau. ([installation de pêche au carrelet]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957098 .Subheading}

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
gaspereau except by angling or with a dip net, gill net, square net,
trap net or weir.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957100 .Subheading}

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall engage in recreational fishing for gaspereau in the
  waters set out in column I of Schedule V

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the yearly
    close time set out in column III; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the weekly
    close time set out in column IV.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in fishing for commercial
  purposes for gaspereau in the waters set out in column I of Schedule V

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the yearly
    close time set out in column III; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the weekly
    close time set out in column IV.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 15
:::

### [Quotas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957110 .Subheading}

**[[41.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41.1 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for gaspereau by any method in the waters set out
in column I of the table to this section shall catch and retain, in any
day, more gaspereau than the fishing quota set out in column II.

### [Mesh Sizes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957120 .Subheading}

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for gaspereau with a gill net that has a mesh size
  of more than 89 mm.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for gaspereau in the inland and
  tidal waters of New Brunswick with a gill net that has a mesh size of
  less than 38 mm.

### [Provisions Specific to Nova Scotia]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957124 .Subheading}

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall fish for gaspereau in the inland waters of Nova Scotia by any
  means other than angling during the period beginning at sunset and
  ending at sunrise.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in the inland waters of
  Halifax and Yarmouth Counties to a person who holds a licence to fish
  for gaspereau with a dip net at a dip stand.

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, in the
inland waters of any river in Nova Scotia,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish for gaspereau with a square net that is more
  than 3.65 m^2^; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} erect or use a square net fixture that extends more
  than one third of the way across the width of the river or that has an
  outside wall of more than 9 m in length.

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** Every holder of a licence
to fish for gaspereau with a square net in the inland waters of a river
in Nova Scotia shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} during the applicable weekly close time for gaspereau
  in those waters set out in column IV of Schedule V, remove the square
  net from the water; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide an opening that is at least 60 cm wide and
  60 cm high in the wall of the square net fixture that extends across
  the river.

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
gaspereau in the inland waters of Inverness County with a trap net that
is more than 15 m in length, measured from the upstream end of the trap
net to the downstream end and including any leader that is attached to
it.

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
gaspereau in the Gaspereau River, Kings County, Nova Scotia,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} with a dip net the opening of which has a
  circumference of more than 190 cm;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} upstream from the mouth of Deep Hollow Brook, except
  by angling or with a dip net or square net;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} from the White Rock power house to a point 640 m
  downstream from that power house; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} from a point 183 m downstream from the Trunk 12
  highway bridge upstream to the Lanes Mill fish ladder.

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
gaspereau in the Tusket River, Nova Scotia, from the Route 423 highway
bridge upstream to the power house.

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Landlocked Salmon]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957141 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957142 .Subheading}

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of recreational fishing for landlocked salmon

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 42](#1375939-1376697){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375939-1376697.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-49-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 49"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957146 .Subheading}

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
landlocked salmon except by angling.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957148 .Subheading}

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
landlocked salmon in the waters set out in column I of an item of the
table to this section during the close time set out in column II of that
item.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957156 .Subheading}

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall catch and retain in any day from the waters set out in column I
  of an item of the table to this subsection

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} more landlocked salmon than the fishing quota set
    out in column II of that item; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any landlocked salmon the length of which is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} less than the minimum length set out in column
      III of that item, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} more than the maximum length set out in column
      IV of that item.

## [PART VI.1]{.HLabel1}[Muskellunge]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957174 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957175 .Subheading}

**[[53.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.1 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply in respect of recreational fishing for muskellunge

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 17
:::

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957181 .Subheading}

**[[53.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for muskellunge except by angling.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 17
:::

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957185 .Subheading}

**[[53.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.3 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for muskellunge in the waters set out in column I of the table to this
section during the close time set out in column II.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957195 .Subheading}

**[[53.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53.4 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch
and retain in any day from the waters set out in column I of the table
to section 53.3

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} more muskellunge than the fishing quota set out in
  column III; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any muskellunge the length of which is less than the
  minimum length set out in column IV, or more than the maximum length
  set out in column V.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 17
:::

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Mussels]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957201 .Part}

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957202 .Subheading}

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
mussels except by hand, with hand-held tools, with a drag rake,
hydraulic device or mechanical device or by diving.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957204 .Subheading}

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall engage in recreational fishing for mussels in the waters
  set out in column I of the table to this section by a method set out
  in column II during the close time set out in column III.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in fishing for commercial
  purposes for mussels in the waters set out in column I of the table to
  this section by a method set out in column II during the close time
  set out in column III.

### [Quotas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957216 .Subheading}

**[[55.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55.1 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for mussels by any method in the waters set out in
column I of the table to this section shall catch and retain, in any
day, more mussels than the fishing quota set out in column II.

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Oysters]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957226 .Part}

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957227 .Subheading}

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
oysters

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by diving in a leased oyster area in the inland or
  tidal waters of Prince Edward Island; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a public oyster-fishing area, except with tongs or
  rakes that are operated by hand without mechanical assistance.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957231 .Subheading}

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, unless
authorized under a licence set out in item 7 of the table to section 5,
fish for oysters in an area set out in column I of the table to this
section

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II of that item during
  the yearly close time set out in column III of that item; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} during the weekly close time set out in column IV of
  that item.

### [Length Restriction]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957243 .Subheading}

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain or possess an oyster that is less than 76 mm in length.

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[Salmon]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957245 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957246 .Subheading}

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[Salmon Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Salmon Fishing Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Salmon Fishing
    Area illustrated and enumerated in Schedule VI and includes the
    waters of any province that flow into such an Area. ([zone de pêche
    du saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957249 .Subheading}

**[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
salmon except by angling with an artificial fly.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957251 .Subheading}

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
66(1), no person shall fish for salmon in the waters set out in column I
of an item of Schedule VII during the close time set out in column II of
that item.

### [Quotas for Specific Waters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957253 .Subheading}

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain from the waters set out in column I of an item of Schedule VII

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} more salmon in any day than the daily catch and
  retain quota set out in column III of that item; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} more salmon in any year than the yearly fishing quota
  set out in column V of that item.

### [Aggregate Quotas and Length Restrictions by Province]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957257 .Subheading}

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain from the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to
this section

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} more salmon in any day than the daily catch and
  retain quota set out in column II of that item;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} more salmon in any year than the yearly fishing quota
  set out in column IV of that item; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any salmon the length of which is less than the
  minimum length set out in column V of that item.

### [Catch and Release Provisions for Specific Waters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957268 .Subheading}

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, during any day, continue to angle for salmon in the
  waters set out in column I of an item of Schedule VII after that
  person has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} caught and retained from those waters the daily
    catch and retain quota set out in column III of that item; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 66(2), caught and released in
    those waters the daily catch and release quota set out in column IV
    of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, during any year, continue to angle
  for salmon in the waters set out in column I of an item of Schedule
  VII after that person has caught and retained from those waters the
  yearly fishing quota set out in column V of that item.

### [Catch and Release Provisions by Province]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957274 .Subheading}

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, during any day, continue to angle for salmon in the
  waters set out in column I of an item of the table to section 63 after
  that person has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} caught and retained from those waters the daily
    catch and retain quota set out in column II of that item; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 66(2), caught and released in
    those waters the daily catch and release quota set out in column III
    of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, during any year, continue to angle
  for salmon in the waters set out in column I of an item of the table
  to section 63 after that person has caught and retained from those
  waters the yearly fishing quota set out in column IV of that item.

<!-- -->

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person may, during the period beginning on April 15 and ending on May
  15, fish for or catch and retain salmon from the inland and tidal
  waters of New Brunswick other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Restigouche River upstream from
    the J.C. Van Horne Bridge in Campbellton; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Nepisiguit River upstream from a
    straight line drawn from grid reference 01487695 to grid reference
    01157740 as found on the map Bathurst 21 P/12.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person who is fishing for salmon pursuant to
  subsection (1) has not caught and retained the applicable daily or
  yearly fishing quota for salmon, the person may catch and release any
  number of salmon in any day.

### [Tagging]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957285 .Subheading}

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person who catches and retains a salmon in accordance with these
  Regulations shall forthwith securely lock or seal on the salmon a
  salmon tag, as specified in subsection (2), that has been issued to
  that person and shall do so in accordance with the design of the tag
  and so that it cannot be removed without tampering with the lock or
  seal, breaking or cutting the tag or cutting or tearing any part of
  the salmon.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A salmon tag that is affixed to a salmon shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a blue tag, where the salmon was caught and
    retained by angling, and shall be a tag specific to a salmon 63 cm
    or more in length where the salmon is 63 cm or more in length; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a brown tag, where the salmon was caught and
    retained under a written permission referred to in section 4 of the
    Act or was taken from an aquaculture area or site or a privately
    owned fish pond.

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/93-335, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-335, s. 6
:::

**[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import a
salmon into a province unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an import tag issued with a licence to import salmon
  set out in item 9 of the table to section 5 is affixed to that salmon
  in the manner described in subsection 67(1) prior to its entry into
  the province or immediately upon its entry into the province; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it bears a salmon tag.

**[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** No person shall have on
board any vessel for which a registration card has been issued any
salmon to which a blue salmon tag is affixed.

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall possess a salmon unless a salmon tag is affixed thereto.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who is
  cooking or smoking a salmon if the tag that was removed from it at the
  time cooking or smoking commenced is readily available for inspection
  by a fishery officer or fishery guardian.

<!-- -->

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), where a
  salmon is caught by angling and retained, the person who hooked the
  salmon, whether or not that person landed the salmon, shall be
  regarded as having caught and retained it, and shall tag it in
  accordance with section 67.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a fishing guide who is authorized to provide
  guide services under an Act of the legislature of a province hooks a
  salmon on behalf of a person who has a licence to fish for salmon and
  who has paid the fishing guide for guide services, subsection (1) does
  not apply to the fishing guide and the person shall, for the purposes
  of that subsection, be considered to have hooked the salmon.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-441, s. 1
:::

**[[71.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71.1 .sectionLabel}** Sections 67, 70 and
71 do not apply in respect of salmon caught under the authority of a
licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-335, s. 7
:::

## [PART X]{.HLabel1}[Shad]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957309 .Part}

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957310 .Subheading}

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
shad except by angling or with a dip net, gill net, square net or trap
net.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 21(F)
:::

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957314 .Subheading}

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall engage in recreational fishing for shad in the waters set
  out in column I of Schedule VIII

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the yearly
    close time set out in column III; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the weekly
    close time set out in column IV.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in fishing for commercial
  purposes for shad in the waters set out in column I of Schedule VIII

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the yearly
    close time set out in column III; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II during the weekly
    close time set out in column IV.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for shad in the inland waters of
  Nova Scotia by any means other than angling during the period
  beginning at sunset and ending at sunrise.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 22
:::

### [Quotas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957325 .Subheading}

**[[73.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.1 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for shad by any method in the waters set out in
column I of the table to this section shall catch and retain, in any
day, more shad than the fishing quota set out in column II.

- SOR/2001-452, s. 23
:::

### [Mesh Size]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957335 .Subheading}

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
shad with a gill net that has a mesh size of less than 127 mm.

## [PART XI]{.HLabel1}[Silversides]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957337 .Part}

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957338 .Subheading}

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
silversides except by angling or with a dip net or trap net.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957340 .Subheading}

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
silversides in the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to
this section by a method set out in column II of that item during the
close time set out in column III of that item.

### [Mesh Size]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957348 .Subheading}

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
silversides with a trap net that has a mesh size of more than 65 mm.

## [PART XII]{.HLabel1}[Smallmouth Bass]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957350 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957351 .Subheading}

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of recreational fishing for smallmouth bass

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957355 .Subheading}

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
smallmouth bass except by angling.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957357 .Subheading}

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
smallmouth bass in the waters set out in column I of an item of the
table to this section during the close time set out in column II of that
item.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957367 .Subheading}

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain in any day from the waters set out in column I of an item of the
table to section 80

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} more smallmouth bass than the fishing quota set out
  in column III of that item; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any smallmouth bass the length of which is less than
  the minimum length set out in column IV, or more than the maximum
  length set out in column V.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 25
:::

## [PART XIII]{.HLabel1}[Smelt]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957373 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957374 .Subheading}

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of recreational fishing for smelt

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957378 .Subheading}

**[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
smelt except by angling or with a bag net, box net, dip net, gill net or
spear.

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
smelt with a bag net, box net or gill net that has a mesh size of less
than 31 mm.

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
smelt with a bag net or box net having a leader that is more than 31 m
in length.

**[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
smelt in the inland or tidal waters of Prince Edward Island with a bag
net that has a leader.

### [Quotas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957383 .Subheading}

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
recreational fishing for smelt with a dip net, by angling, or with a
spear shall catch and retain more than 60 smelt in any day.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 26
:::

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957387 .Subheading}

**[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
smelt in the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to this
section by a method set out in column II of that item during the close
time set out in column III of that item.

## [PART XIV]{.HLabel1}[Striped Bass]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957397 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957398 .Subheading}

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of recreational fishing for striped bass by angling or with
set lines in the waters set out in Schedule IX during the period
beginning on January 1 and ending on March 31.

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957400 .Subheading}

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
striped bass except by angling or with a bow net.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957402 .Subheading}

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
striped bass in the waters set out in column I of an item of the table
to this section

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a method set out in column II of that item during
  the yearly close time set out in column III of that item; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} with a bow net during the weekly close time set out
  in column IV of that item.

### [Mesh Size]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957412 .Subheading}

**[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
striped bass with a bow net that has a mesh size of less than 127 mm.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957414 .Subheading}

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain from the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to
this section by a method set out in column II of that item

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} more striped bass in any day than the fishing quota
  set out in column III of that item; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any striped bass the length of which is less than the
  minimum length set out in column IV, or more than the maximum length
  set out in column V.
