## [PART XV]{.HLabel1}[Sturgeon]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957426 .Part}

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957427 .Subheading}

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
sturgeon except by angling or with a gill net.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957429 .Subheading}

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
sturgeon in the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to
this section by a method set out in column II of that item during the
close time set out in column III of that item.

### [Mesh Size]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957437 .Subheading}

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
sturgeon with a gill net that has a mesh size of less than 330 mm.

### [Length Restriction]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957439 .Subheading}

**[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain or possess any sturgeon that is less than 120 cm in length.

## [PART XVI]{.HLabel1}[Tomcod]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957441 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957442 .Subheading}

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply
in respect of recreational fishing for tomcod by angling or with set
lines in the waters set out in Schedule IX during the period beginning
on January 1 and ending on March 31.

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957444 .Subheading}

**[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
tomcod except by angling or with a bag net, box net, gill net or spear.

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for tomcod with a bag net, box net or gill net that has a mesh size of
less than 31 mm.

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for tomcod with a bag net or box net having a leader that is more than
31 m in length.

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for tomcod in the inland and tidal waters of Prince Edward Island with a
bag net that has a leader.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957449 .Subheading}

**[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for tomcod in the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to
this section by a method set out in column II of that item during the
close time set out in column III of that item.

## [PART XVII]{.HLabel1}[Trout]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957457 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957458 .Subheading}

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply in respect of recreational fishing for trout

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957462 .Subheading}

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for trout except by angling.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957464 .Subheading}

**[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
for trout in the waters set out in column I of an item of the table to
this section during the close time set out in column II of that item.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957472 .Subheading}

- **[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall catch and retain from the waters set out in column I of
  the table to this section

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any day, more trout of a species than the
    fishing quota for that species set out in column II; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trout of a species set out in column II the
    length of which is less than the minimum length for that species set
    out in column III or more than the maximum length for that species
    set out in column IV.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall catch and retain in any day more than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} five trout in the aggregate from any combination of
    the waters set out in column 1 of items 1 and 2 of the table to this
    section; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 10 trout in the aggregate from the waters set out
    in column 1 of item 3 of the table to this section.

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(4)</span> 100 cm</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-55, err.(F), Vol. 127, No. 16
- SOR/2001-452, s. 29
:::

## [PART XVII.1]{.HLabel1}[Whitefish]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957488 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957489 .Subheading}

**[[107.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107.1 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply in respect of recreational fishing for whitefish

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by angling or with set lines in the waters set out in
  Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1 and ending on
  March 31; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in section 116.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 29
:::

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957495 .Subheading}

**[[107.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107.11 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
fish for whitefish except by angling.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-452, s. 29
:::

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957499 .Subheading}

**[[107.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107.12 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
fish for whitefish in the waters set out in column I of the table to
this section during the close time set out in column II.

## [PART XVIII]{.HLabel1}[Winter Fishing in New Brunswick]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957569 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957570 .Subheading}

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of recreational fishing by angling and with set lines in the
waters set out in Schedule IX during the period beginning on January 1
and ending on March 31.

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957572 .Subheading}

**[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
except

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} through a man-made hole in the ice; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} from shore through a natural opening in the ice.

<!-- -->

- **[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish with more than five set lines.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who fishes with set lines shall attend
  those lines and maintain an unobstructed view of those lines.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957579 .Subheading}

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish
during the period beginning on March 30 and ending on March 31.

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish in
Lac Baker, Madawaska County, during the period beginning on Monday and
ending on Friday in any week.

**[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, except
in the waters set out in items 4, 7, 13, 14, 16, 20, 21, 28 and 60 of
Schedule IX, fish during the period beginning one-half hour after sunset
and ending one-half hour before sunrise.

### [Quotas and Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957583 .Subheading}

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall catch and retain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any day more fish of a species set out in column
    I of an item of the table to this subsection than the fishing quota
    set out in column II of that item; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any fish of a species set out in column I of an
    item of the table to this subsection the length of which is less
    than the minimum length set out in column III of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall catch and retain in any day more
  sport fish and striped bass in the aggregate than the lesser of five
  fish and 3.5 kg plus one fish.

### [Provisions Specific to Nictau Lake]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957595 .Subheading}

**[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** No person who fishes in
Nictau Lake, Restigouche County, shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish with more than one set line;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} use any fish as bait;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} catch and retain any landlocked salmon that is less
  than 50 cm in length or more than 63 cm in length;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} catch and retain any brook trout that is less than
  25 cm in length;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in any day, catch and retain more than one landlocked
  salmon; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} continue to fish in that lake on any day after
  catching and retaining two sport fish in that day.

## [PART XIX]{.HLabel1}[Boundary Waters Between New Brunswick and the State of Maine]{.HTitleText1} {#h-957603 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957604 .Subheading}

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of recreational fishing for smelt, whitefish and sport fish,
other than salmon, in the following inland waters of New Brunswick that
border on waters in the State of Maine:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the St. Francis River and Glasier Lake in Madawaska
  County and that portion of the Saint John River in Madawaska County;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Monument Brook, North Lake, the Thoroughfare between
  North Lake and (East) Grand Lake, (East) Grand Lake, Mud Lake, the St.
  Croix River, Spednic Lake and Palfrey Lake in York County; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the St. Croix River, Grand Falls Flowage and Woodland
  Flowage in Charlotte County.

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957609 .Subheading}

- **[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish for sport fish or whitefish except by angling;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fish for smelt except by angling or with a dip net.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who fishes in accordance with subsection
  119(2) may fish with set lines.

<!-- -->

- **[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish with more than five set lines.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who fishes with set lines shall attend
  those lines and maintain an unobstructed view of those lines.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957618 .Subheading}

- **[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall fish during the period beginning on October 1 and ending on
  April 14.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may, during the period beginning on January
  1 and ending on March 31, fish through a man-made hole in the ice in
  Glasier Lake, North Lake, the Thoroughfare between North Lake and
  (East) Grand Lake, (East) Grand Lake, Mud Lake, Spednic Lake, Palfrey
  Lake, Grand Falls Flowage or Woodland Flowage.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, during the period beginning on
  January 1 and ending on March 31, fish during the period beginning
  one-half hour after sunset and ending one-half hour before sunrise.

### [Daily Quotas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957623 .Subheading}

- **[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, during the period beginning on April 1 and ending on
  December 31, catch and retain in any day more fish of a species set
  out in column I of an item of the table to this section than the daily
  fishing quota set out in column II of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, during the period beginning on April
  1 and ending on December 31, catch and retain in any day more sport
  fish in the aggregate than the lesser of five fish and 3.5 kg plus one
  fish.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, during the period beginning on
  January 1 and ending on March 31, catch and retain in any day more
  fish of a species set out in column I of an item of the table to this
  section than the daily fishing quota set out in column III of that
  item.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, during the period beginning on
  January 1 and ending on March 31, catch and retain in any day more
  sport fish in the aggregate than the lesser of three fish and 3.5 kg
  plus one fish.

### [Length Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-957637 .Subheading}

**[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch
and retain a fish of a species set out in column I of an item of the
table to section 120 the length of which is less than the minimum length
set out in column IV of that item.

:::::: section
::::: {#957639 .Schedule}
::: {}
